#!/usr/bin/env node

import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const CONFIG = {
  url: process.env.DEBOT_URL || "https://debot.ai/?chain=solana",
  chromePort: Number(process.env.CHROME_DEBUG_PORT || 9222),
  chromeProfileDir:
    process.env.CHROME_DEBUG_PROFILE ||
    path.join(process.cwd(), ".debot-chrome-profile"),
  pollMs: Number(process.env.DEBOT_POLL_MS || 20_000),
  summaryMs: Number(process.env.DEBOT_SUMMARY_MS || 10 * 60_000),
  statePath:
    process.env.DEBOT_STATE_PATH ||
    path.join(process.cwd(), ".debot-monitor-state.json"),
  telegramToken: process.env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN || "",
  telegramChatId: process.env.TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID || "",
  dryRun: process.env.DEBOT_DRY_RUN === "1",
};

const DOG_GRADES = [
  ["金狗", /gold|金狗|黄金|金/i],
  ["银狗", /silver|银狗|白银|银/i],
  ["铜狗", /bronze|铜狗|青铜|铜/i],
];

const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));

async function requestJson(url, options = {}) {
  const response = await fetch(url, options);
  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`${response.status} ${response.statusText}: ${await response.text()}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}

async function loadState() {
  if (!existsSync(CONFIG.statePath)) {
    return { seen: {}, lastSummaryAt: Date.now() };
  }
  try {
    return JSON.parse(await readFile(CONFIG.statePath, "utf8"));
  } catch {
    return { seen: {}, lastSummaryAt: 0 };
  }
}

async function saveState(state) {
  await mkdir(path.dirname(CONFIG.statePath), { recursive: true });
  await writeFile(CONFIG.statePath, JSON.stringify(state, null, 2));
}

function chromeExecutable() {
  const candidates = [
    "/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome",
    "/Appl<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1133" />byCa = new Map();
  for (const link of tokenLinks) {
    const href = new URL(link.getAttribute('href'), location.href).href;
    const match = href.match(tokenRe);
    if (!match) continue;
    const ca = match[1];
    const card = link.closest('[role="link"], [class*="card"], [class*="signal"], [class*="token"], [class]') || link.parentElement;
    const text = (card?.innerText || link.innerText || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    const project = findName(link, ca);
    const imgAlts = [...(card || document).querySelectorAll('img')]
      .map((img) => (img.alt || '') + ' ' + (img.getAttribute('src') || ''))
      .join(' ');
    const debotHref = href.includes('debot.ai/token/')
      ? href
      : 'https://debot.ai/token/solana/306326_' + ca;
    byCa.set(ca, { project, ca, href: debotHref, text, imgAlts });
  }
  const current = location.href.match(/306326_([A-Za-z0-9]+)/);
  if (current && !byCa.has(current[1])) {
    const ca = current[1];
    byCa.set(ca, {
      project: document.querySelector('h1')?.innerText?.trim() || ca.slice(0, 6),
      ca,
      href: location.href,
      text: document.body.innerText.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 2000),
      imgAlts: [...document.querySelectorAll('img')].map((img) => (img.alt || '') + ' ' + (img.getAttribute('src') || '')).join(' '),
    });
  }
  return [...byCa.values()].slice(0, 80);
})()
`;

function classifySignal(item) {
  const haystack = `${item.text} ${item.imgAlts}`;
  for (const [grade, re] of DOG_GRADES) {
    if (re.test(haystack)) return grade;
  }
  return "普通信号";
}

function normalizeProject(project) {
  return project
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .replace(/\s+[+-]\d[\d.,]*%.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/\s+MC\s+\$.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/\s+TXs\s+.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/[：:]+$/, "")
    .trim();
}

function formatLine(item) {
  return `${normalizeProject(item.project)} ${item.grade} ${item.ca} ${item.href}`;
}

async function fetchSignals() {
  await ensureChrome();

  await ensureChrome();
  const target = await getDebotTarget();
  const client = new CdpClient(target.webSocketDebuggerUrl);
  await client.connect();
  try {
    await client.call("Runtime.enable");
    await client.call("Page.bringToFront").catch(() => {});
    await client.call("Runtime.evaluate", {
      expression: `location.href.includes('debot.ai') || (location.href = ${JSON.stringify(CONFIG.url)})`,
    });
    await sleep(4000);
    const result = await client.call("Runtime.evaluate", {

      returnByValue: true,
      awaitPromise: true,
    });
    return (result.result.value || []).map((item) => ({
      ...item,
      grade: classifySignal(item),
      seenAt: Date.now(),
    }));
  } finally {
    client.close();
  }
}

async function sendTelegram(text) {
  if (CONFIG.dryRun || !CONFIG.telegramToken || !CONFIG.telegramChatId) {
    console.log(text);
    return;
  }
  const url = `https://api.telegram.org/bot${CONFIG.telegramToken}/sendMessage`;
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      chat_id: CONFIG.telegramChatId,
      text,
      disable_web_page_preview: true,
    }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Telegram send failed: ${response.status} ${await response.text()}`);
  }
}

function pickNewSignals(signals, state) {
  return signals.filter((item) => !state.seen[item.ca]);
}

async function sendNewSignals(signals, state) {
  const fresh = pickNewSignals(signals, state);
  if (fresh.length === 0) return false;
  const text = fresh.map(formatLine).join("\n");
  await sendTelegram(text);
  for (const item of fresh) {
    state.seen[item.ca] = {
      project: item.project,
      grade: item.grade,
      href: item.href,
      firstSeenAt: Date.now(),
    };
  }
  return true;
}

async function sendSummaryIfDue(signals, state) {
  const now = Date.now();
  if (now - Number(state.lastSummaryAt || 0) < CONFIG.summaryMs) return;
  state.lastSummaryAt = now;
  const top = signals.slice(0, 20);
  const text =
    top.length > 0
      ? top.map(formatLine).join("\n")
      : "暂无新的金狗/银狗/铜狗/普通信号。";
  await sendTelegram(text);
}

async function tick(state) {
  const signals = await fetchSignals();
  const sentFresh = await sendNewSignals(signals, state);
  if (sentFresh && !state.lastSummaryAt) {
    state.lastSummaryAt = Date.now();
  }
  await sendSummaryIfDue(signals, state);
  await saveState(state);
  console.log(`[${new Date().toISOString()}] scanned=${signals.length}`);
}

async function main() {
  const state = await loadState();
  console.log(`DeBot monitor started. poll=${CONFIG.pollMs}ms summary=${CONFIG.summaryMs}ms`);
  while (true) {
    try {
      await tick(state);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`[${new Date().toISOString()}] ${error.stack || error.message}`);
    }
    await sleep(CONFIG.pollMs);
  }
}

main();

#!/usr/bin/env node

import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const CONFIG = {
  url: process.env.DEBOT_URL || "https://debot.ai/?chain=solana",
  chromePort: Number(process.env.CHROME_DEBUG_PORT || 9222),
  chromeProfileDir:
    process.env.CHROME_DEBUG_PROFILE ||
    path.join(process.cwd(), ".debot-chrome-profile"),
  pollMs: Number(process.env.DEBOT_POLL_MS || 20_000),
  summaryMs: Number(process.env.DEBOT_SUMMARY_MS || 10 * 60_000),
  statePath:
    process.env.DEBOT_STATE_PATH ||
    path.join(process.cwd(), ".debot-monitor-state.json"),
  telegramToken: process.env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN || "",
  telegramChatId: process.env.TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID || "",
  dryRun: process.env.DEBOT_DRY_RUN === "1",
};

const DOG_GRADES = [
  ["金狗", /gold|金狗|黄金|金/i],
  ["银狗", /silver|银狗|白银|银/i],
  ["铜狗", /bronze|铜狗|青铜|铜/i],
];

const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));

async function requestJson(url, options = {}) {
  const response = await fetch(url, options);
  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`${response.status} ${response.statusText}: ${await response.text()}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}

async function loadState() {
  if (!existsSync(CONFIG.statePath)) {
    return { seen: {}, lastSummaryAt: Date.now() };
  }
  try {
    return JSON.parse(await readFile(CONFIG.statePath, "utf8"));
  } catch {
    return { seen: {}, lastSummaryAt: 0 };
  }
}

async function saveState(state) {
  await mkdir(path.dirname(CONFIG.statePath), { recursive: true });
  await writeFile(CONFIG.statePath, JSON.stringify(state, null, 2));
}

function chromeExecutable() {
  const candidates = [
    "/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome",
    "/Appl<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1133" />byCa = new Map();
  for (const link of tokenLinks) {
    const href = new URL(link.getAttribute('href'), location.href).href;
    const match = href.match(tokenRe);
    if (!match) continue;
    const ca = match[1];
    const card = link.closest('[role="link"], [class*="card"], [class*="signal"], [class*="token"], [class]') || link.parentElement;
    const text = (card?.innerText || link.innerText || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    const project = findName(link, ca);
    const imgAlts = [...(card || document).querySelectorAll('img')]
      .map((img) => (img.alt || '') + ' ' + (img.getAttribute('src') || ''))
      .join(' ');
    const debotHref = href.includes('debot.ai/token/')
      ? href
      : 'https://debot.ai/token/solana/306326_' + ca;
    byCa.set(ca, { project, ca, href: debotHref, text, imgAlts });
  }
  const current = location.href.match(/306326_([A-Za-z0-9]+)/);
  if (current && !byCa.has(current[1])) {
    const ca = current[1];
    byCa.set(ca, {
      project: document.querySelector('h1')?.innerText?.trim() || ca.slice(0, 6),
      ca,
      href: location.href,
      text: document.body.innerText.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 2000),
      imgAlts: [...document.querySelectorAll('img')].map((img) => (img.alt || '') + ' ' + (img.getAttribute('src') || '')).join(' '),
    });
  }
  return [...byCa.values()].slice(0, 80);
})()
`;

function classifySignal(item) {
  const haystack = `${item.text} ${item.imgAlts}`;
  for (const [grade, re] of DOG_GRADES) {
    if (re.test(haystack)) return grade;
  }
  return "普通信号";
}

function normalizeProject(project) {
  return project
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .replace(/\s+[+-]\d[\d.,]*%.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/\s+MC\s+\$.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/\s+TXs\s+.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/[：:]+$/, "")
    .trim();
}

function formatLine(item) {
  return `${normalizeProject(item.project)} ${item.grade} ${item.ca} ${item.href}`;
}

async function fetchSignals() {
  await ensureChrome();

  await ensureChrome();
  const target = await getDebotTarget();
  const client = new CdpClient(target.webSocketDebuggerUrl);
  await client.connect();
  try {
    await client.call("Runtime.enable");
    await client.call("Page.bringToFront").catch(() => {});
    await client.call("Runtime.evaluate", {
      expression: `location.href.includes('debot.ai') || (location.href = ${JSON.stringify(CONFIG.url)})`,
    });
    await sleep(4000);
    const result = await client.call("Runtime.evaluate", {
      expression: extractionScript,
      returnByValue: true,
      awaitPromise: true,
    });
    return (result.result.value || []).map((item) => ({
      ...item,
      grade: classifySignal(item),
      seenAt: Date.now(),
    }));
  } finally {
    client.close();
  }
}

async function sendTelegram(text) {
  if (CONFIG.dryRun || !CONFIG.telegramToken || !CONFIG.telegramChatId) {
    console.log(text);
    return;
  }
  const url = `https://api.telegram.org/bot${CONFIG.telegramToken}/sendMessage`;
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      chat_id: CONFIG.telegramChatId,
      text,
      disable_web_page_preview: true,
    }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Telegram send failed: ${response.status} ${await response.text()}`);
  }
}

function pickNewSignals(signals, state) {
  return signals.filter((item) => !state.seen[item.ca]);
}

async function sendNewSignals(signals, state) {
  const fresh = pickNewSignals(signals, state);
  if (fresh.length === 0) return false;
  const text = fresh.map(formatLine).join("\n");
  await sendTelegram(text);
  for (const item of fresh) {
    state.seen[item.ca] = {
      project: item.project,
      grade: item.grade,
      href: item.href,
      firstSeenAt: Date.now(),
    };
  }
  return true;
}

async function sendSummaryIfDue(signals, state) {
  const now = Date.now();
  if (now - Number(state.lastSummaryAt || 0) < CONFIG.summaryMs) return;
  state.lastSummaryAt = now;
  const top = signals.slice(0, 20);
  const text =
    top.length > 0
      ? top.map(formatLine).join("\n")
      : "暂无新的金狗/银狗/铜狗/普通信号。";
  await sendTelegram(text);
}

async function tick(state) {
  const signals = await fetchSignals();
  const sentFresh = await sendNewSignals(signals, state);
  if (sentFresh && !state.lastSummaryAt) {
    state.lastSummaryAt = Date.now();
  }
  await sendSummaryIfDue(signals, state);
  await saveState(state);
  console.log(`[${new Date().toISOString()}] scanned=${signals.length}`);
}

async function main() {
  const state = await loadState();
  console.log(`DeBot monitor started. poll=${CONFIG.pollMs}ms summary=${CONFIG.summaryMs}ms`);
  while (true) {
    try {
      await tick(state);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`[${new Date().toISOString()}] ${error.stack || error.message}`);
    }
    await sleep(CONFIG.pollMs);
  }
}

main();

#!/usr/bin/env node

import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const CONFIG = {
  url: process.env.DEBOT_URL || "https://debot.ai/?chain=solana",
  chromePort: Number(process.env.CHROME_DEBUG_PORT || 9222),
  chromeProfileDir:
    process.env.CHROME_DEBUG_PROFILE ||
    path.join(process.cwd(), ".debot-chrome-profile"),
  pollMs: Number(process.env.DEBOT_POLL_MS || 20_000),
  summaryMs: Number(process.env.DEBOT_SUMMARY_MS || 10 * 60_000),
  statePath:
    process.env.DEBOT_STATE_PATH ||
    path.join(process.cwd(), ".debot-monitor-state.json"),
  telegramToken: process.env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN || "",
  telegramChatId: process.env.TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID || "",
  dryRun: process.env.DEBOT_DRY_RUN === "1",
};

const DOG_GRADES = [
  ["金狗", /gold|金狗|黄金|金/i],
  ["银狗", /silver|银狗|白银|银/i],
  ["铜狗", /bronze|铜狗|青铜|铜/i],
];

const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));

async function requestJson(url, options = {}) {
  const response = await fetch(url, options);
  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`${response.status} ${response.statusText}: ${await response.text()}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}

async function loadState() {
  if (!existsSync(CONFIG.statePath)) {
    return { seen: {}, lastSummaryAt: Date.now() };
  }
  try {
    return JSON.parse(await readFile(CONFIG.statePath, "utf8"));
  } catch {
    return { seen: {}, lastSummaryAt: 0 };
  }
}

async function saveState(state) {
  await mkdir(path.dirname(CONFIG.statePath), { recursive: true });
  await writeFile(CONFIG.statePath, JSON.stringify(state, null, 2));
}

function chromeExecutable() {
  const candidates = [
    "/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome",
    "/Appl<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1133" />byCa = new Map();
  for (const link of tokenLinks) {
    const href = new URL(link.getAttribute('href'), location.href).href;
    const match = href.match(tokenRe);
    if (!match) continue;
    const ca = match[1];
    const card = link.closest('[role="link"], [class*="card"], [class*="signal"], [class*="token"], [class]') || link.parentElement;
    const text = (card?.innerText || link.innerText || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    const project = findName(link, ca);
    const imgAlts = [...(card || document).querySelectorAll('img')]
      .map((img) => (img.alt || '') + ' ' + (img.getAttribute('src') || ''))
      .join(' ');
    const debotHref = href.includes('debot.ai/token/')
      ? href
      : 'https://debot.ai/token/solana/306326_' + ca;
    byCa.set(ca, { project, ca, href: debotHref, text, imgAlts });
  }
  const current = location.href.match(/306326_([A-Za-z0-9]+)/);
  if (current && !byCa.has(current[1])) {
    const ca = current[1];
    byCa.set(ca, {
      project: document.querySelector('h1')?.innerText?.trim() || ca.slice(0, 6),
      ca,
      href: location.href,
      text: document.body.innerText.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 2000),
      imgAlts: [...document.querySelectorAll('img')].map((img) => (img.alt || '') + ' ' + (img.getAttribute('src') || '')).join(' '),
    });
  }
  return [...byCa.values()].slice(0, 80);
})()
`;

function classifySignal(item) {
  const haystack = `${item.text} ${item.imgAlts}`;
  for (const [grade, re] of DOG_GRADES) {
    if (re.test(haystack)) return grade;
  }
  return "普通信号";
}

function normalizeProject(project) {
  return project
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .replace(/\s+[+-]\d[\d.,]*%.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/\s+MC\s+\$.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/\s+TXs\s+.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/[：:]+$/, "")
    .trim();
}

function formatLine(item) {
  return `${normalizeProject(item.project)} ${item.grade} ${item.ca} ${item.href}`;
}

async function fetchSignals() {
  await ensureChrome();

  await ensureChrome();
  const target = await getDebotTarget();
  const client = new CdpClient(target.webSocketDebuggerUrl);
  await client.connect();
  try {
    await client.call("Runtime.enable");
    await client.call("Page.bringToFront").catch(() => {});
    await client.call("Runtime.evaluate", {
      expression: `location.href.includes('debot.ai') || (location.href = ${JSON.stringify(CONFIG.url)})`,
    });
    await sleep(4000);
    const result = await client.call("Runtime.evaluate", {
      expression: extractionScript,
      returnByValue: true,
      awaitPromise: true,
    });
    return (result.result.value || []).map((item) => ({
      ...item,
      grade: classifySignal(item),
      seenAt: Date.now(),
    }));
  } finally {
    client.close();
  }
}

async function sendTelegram(text) {
  if (CONFIG.dryRun || !CONFIG.telegramToken || !CONFIG.telegramChatId) {
    console.log(text);
    return;
  }
  const url = `https://api.telegram.org/bot${CONFIG.telegramToken}/sendMessage`;
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      chat_id: CONFIG.telegramChatId,
      text,
      disable_web_page_preview: true,
    }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Telegram send failed: ${response.status} ${await response.text()}`);
  }
}

function pickNewSignals(signals, state) {
  return signals.filter((item) => !state.seen[item.ca]);
}

async function sendNewSignals(signals, state) {
  const fresh = pickNewSignals(signals, state);
  if (fresh.length === 0) return false;
  const text = fresh.map(formatLine).join("\n");
  await sendTelegram(text);
  for (const item of fresh) {
    state.seen[item.ca] = {
      project: item.project,
      grade: item.grade,
      href: item.href,
      firstSeenAt: Date.now(),
    };
  }
  return true;
}

async function sendSummaryIfDue(signals, state) {
  const now = Date.now();
  if (now - Number(state.lastSummaryAt || 0) < CONFIG.summaryMs) return;
  state.lastSummaryAt = now;
  const top = signals.slice(0, 20);
  const text =
    top.length > 0
      ? top.map(formatLine).join("\n")
      : "暂无新的金狗/银狗/铜狗/普通信号。";
  await sendTelegram(text);
}

async function tick(state) {
  const signals = await fetchSignals();
  const sentFresh = await sendNewSignals(signals, state);
  if (sentFresh && !state.lastSummaryAt) {
    state.lastSummaryAt = Date.now();
  }
  await sendSummaryIfDue(signals, state);
  await saveState(state);
  console.log(`[${new Date().toISOString()}] scanned=${signals.length}`);
}

async function main() {
  const state = await loadState();
  console.log(`DeBot monitor started. poll=${CONFIG.pollMs}ms summary=${CONFIG.summaryMs}ms`);
  while (true) {
    try {
      await tick(state);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`[${new Date().toISOString()}] ${error.stack || error.message}`);
    }
    await sleep(CONFIG.pollMs);
  }
}

main();

#!/usr/bin/env node

import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const CONFIG = {
  url: process.env.DEBOT_URL || "https://debot.ai/?chain=solana",
  chromePort: Number(process.env.CHROME_DEBUG_PORT || 9222),
  chromeProfileDir:
    process.env.CHROME_DEBUG_PROFILE ||
    path.join(process.cwd(), ".debot-chrome-profile"),
  pollMs: Number(process.env.DEBOT_POLL_MS || 20_000),
  summaryMs: Number(process.env.DEBOT_SUMMARY_MS || 10 * 60_000),
  statePath:
    process.env.DEBOT_STATE_PATH ||
    path.join(process.cwd(), ".debot-monitor-state.json"),
  telegramToken: process.env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN || "",
  telegramChatId: process.env.TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID || "",
  dryRun: process.env.DEBOT_DRY_RUN === "1",
};

const DOG_GRADES = [
  ["金狗", /gold|金狗|黄金|金/i],
  ["银狗", /silver|银狗|白银|银/i],
  ["铜狗", /bronze|铜狗|青铜|铜/i],
];

const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));

async function requestJson(url, options = {}) {
  const response = await fetch(url, options);
  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`${response.status} ${response.statusText}: ${await response.text()}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}

async function loadState() {
  if (!existsSync(CONFIG.statePath)) {
    return { seen: {}, lastSummaryAt: Date.now() };
  }
  try {
    return JSON.parse(await readFile(CONFIG.statePath, "utf8"));
  } catch {
    return { seen: {}, lastSummaryAt: 0 };
  }
}

async function saveState(state) {
  await mkdir(path.dirname(CONFIG.statePath), { recursive: true });
  await writeFile(CONFIG.statePath, JSON.stringify(state, null, 2));
}

function chromeExecutable() {
  const candidates = [
    "/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome",
    "/Appl<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1133" />byCa = new Map();
  for (const link of tokenLinks) {
    const href = new URL(link.getAttribute('href'), location.href).href;
    const match = href.match(tokenRe);
    if (!match) continue;
    const ca = match[1];
    const card = link.closest('[role="link"], [class*="card"], [class*="signal"], [class*="token"], [class]') || link.parentElement;
    const text = (card?.innerText || link.innerText || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    const project = findName(link, ca);
    const imgAlts = [...(card || document).querySelectorAll('img')]
      .map((img) => (img.alt || '') + ' ' + (img.getAttribute('src') || ''))
      .join(' ');
    const debotHref = href.includes('debot.ai/token/')
      ? href
      : 'https://debot.ai/token/solana/306326_' + ca;
    byCa.set(ca, { project, ca, href: debotHref, text, imgAlts });
  }
  const current = location.href.match(/306326_([A-Za-z0-9]+)/);
  if (current && !byCa.has(current[1])) {
    const ca = current[1];
    byCa.set(ca, {
      project: document.querySelector('h1')?.innerText?.trim() || ca.slice(0, 6),
      ca,
      href: location.href,
      text: document.body.innerText.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 2000),
      imgAlts: [...document.querySelectorAll('img')].map((img) => (img.alt || '') + ' ' + (img.getAttribute('src') || '')).join(' '),
    });
  }
  return [...byCa.values()].slice(0, 80);
})()
`;

function classifySignal(item) {
  const haystack = `${item.text} ${item.imgAlts}`;
  for (const [grade, re] of DOG_GRADES) {
    if (re.test(haystack)) return grade;
  }
  return "普通信号";
}

function normalizeProject(project) {
  return project
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .replace(/\s+[+-]\d[\d.,]*%.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/\s+MC\s+\$.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/\s+TXs\s+.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/[：:]+$/, "")
    .trim();
}

function formatLine(item) {
  return `${normalizeProject(item.project)} ${item.grade} ${item.ca} ${item.href}`;
}

async function fetchSignals() {
  await ensureChrome();

  await ensureChrome();
  const target = await getDebotTarget();
  const client = new CdpClient(target.webSocketDebuggerUrl);
  await client.connect();
  try {
    await client.call("Runtime.enable");
    await client.call("Page.bringToFront").catch(() => {});
    await client.call("Runtime.evaluate", {
      expression: `location.href.includes('debot.ai') || (location.href = ${JSON.stringify(CONFIG.url)})`,
    });
    await sleep(4000);
    const result = await client.call("Runtime.evaluate", {
      expression: extractionScript,
      returnByValue: true,
      awaitPromise: true,
    });
    return (result.result.value || []).map((item) => ({
      ...item,
      grade: classifySignal(item),
      seenAt: Date.now(),
    }));
  } finally {
    client.close();
  }
}

async function sendTelegram(text) {
  if (CONFIG.dryRun || !CONFIG.telegramToken || !CONFIG.telegramChatId) {
    console.log(text);
    return;
  }
  const url = `https://api.telegram.org/bot${CONFIG.telegramToken}/sendMessage`;
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      chat_id: CONFIG.telegramChatId,
      text,
      disable_web_page_preview: true,
    }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Telegram send failed: ${response.status} ${await response.text()}`);
  }
}

function pickNewSignals(signals, state) {
  return signals.filter((item) => !state.seen[item.ca]);
}

async function sendNewSignals(signals, state) {
  const fresh = pickNewSignals(signals, state);
  if (fresh.length === 0) return false;
  const text = fresh.map(formatLine).join("\n");
  await sendTelegram(text);
  for (const item of fresh) {
    state.seen[item.ca] = {
      project: item.project,
      grade: item.grade,
      href: item.href,
      firstSeenAt: Date.now(),
    };
  }
  return true;
}

async function sendSummaryIfDue(signals, state) {
  const now = Date.now();
  if (now - Number(state.lastSummaryAt || 0) < CONFIG.summaryMs) return;
  state.lastSummaryAt = now;
  const top = signals.slice(0, 20);
  const text =
    top.length > 0
      ? top.map(formatLine).join("\n")
      : "暂无新的金狗/银狗/铜狗/普通信号。";
  await sendTelegram(text);
}

async function tick(state) {
  const signals = await fetchSignals();
  const sentFresh = await sendNewSignals(signals, state);
  if (sentFresh && !state.lastSummaryAt) {
    state.lastSummaryAt = Date.now();
  }
  await sendSummaryIfDue(signals, state);
  await saveState(state);
  console.log(`[${new Date().toISOString()}] scanned=${signals.length}`);
}

async function main() {
  const state = await loadState();
  console.log(`DeBot monitor started. poll=${CONFIG.pollMs}ms summary=${CONFIG.summaryMs}ms`);
  while (true) {
    try {
      await tick(state);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`[${new Date().toISOString()}] ${error.stack || error.message}`);
    }
    await sleep(CONFIG.pollMs);
  }
}

main();

#!/usr/bin/env node

import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const CONFIG = {
  url: process.env.DEBOT_URL || "https://debot.ai/?chain=solana",
  chromePort: Number(process.env.CHROME_DEBUG_PORT || 9222),
  chromeProfileDir:
    process.env.CHROME_DEBUG_PROFILE ||
    path.join(process.cwd(), ".debot-chrome-profile"),
  pollMs: Number(process.env.DEBOT_POLL_MS || 20_000),
  summaryMs: Number(process.env.DEBOT_SUMMARY_MS || 10 * 60_000),
  statePath:

  dryRun: process.env.DEBOT_DRY_RUN === "1",
};

const DOG_GRADES = [
  ["金狗", /gold|金狗|黄金|金/i],
  ["银狗", /silver|银狗|白银|银/i],
  ["铜狗", /bronze|铜狗|青铜|铜/i],
];

const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));

async function requestJson(url, options = {}) {
  const response = await fetch(url, options);
  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`${response.status} ${response.statusText}: ${await response.text()}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}

async function loadState() {
  if (!existsSync(CONFIG.statePath)) {
    return { seen: {}, lastSummaryAt: Date.now() };
  }
  try {
    return JSON.parse(await readFile(CONFIG.statePath, "utf8"));
  } catch {
    return { seen: {}, lastSummaryAt: 0 };
  }
}

async function saveState(state) {
  await mkdir(path.dirname(CONFIG.statePath), { recursive: true });
  await writeFile(CONFIG.statePath, JSON.stringify(state, null, 2));
}

function chromeExecutable() {
  const candidates = [

    "/Appl<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1133" />byCa = new Map();
  for (const link of tokenLinks) {
    const href = new URL(link.getAttribute('href'), location.href).href;
    const match = href.match(tokenRe);
    if (!match) continue;
    const ca = match[1];
    const card = link.closest('[role="link"], [class*="card"], [class*="signal"], [class*="token"], [class]') || link.parentElement;
    const text = (card?.innerText || link.innerText || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    const project = findName(link, ca);
    const imgAlts = [...(card || document).querySelectorAll('img')]
      .map((img) => (img.alt || '') + ' ' + (img.getAttribute('src') || ''))
      .join(' ');
    const debotHref = href.includes('debot.ai/token/')
      ? href
      : 'https://debot.ai/token/solana/306326_' + ca;
    byCa.set(ca, { project, ca, href: debotHref, text, imgAlts });
  }
  const current = location.href.match(/306326_([A-Za-z0-9]+)/);
  if (current && !byCa.has(current[1])) {
    const ca = current[1];
    byCa.set(ca, {
      project: document.querySelector('h1')?.innerText?.trim() || ca.slice(0, 6),
      ca,
      href: location.href,
      text: document.body.innerText.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 2000),
      imgAlts: [...document.querySelectorAll('img')].map((img) => (img.alt || '') + ' ' + (img.getAttribute('src') || '')).join(' '),
    });
  }
  return [...byCa.values()].slice(0, 80);
})()
`;

function classifySignal(item) {
  const haystack = `${item.text} ${item.imgAlts}`;
  for (const [grade, re] of DOG_GRADES) {
    if (re.test(haystack)) return grade;
  }
  return "普通信号";
}

function normalizeProject(project) {
  return project
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .replace(/\s+[+-]\d[\d.,]*%.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/\s+MC\s+\$.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/\s+TXs\s+.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/[：:]+$/, "")
    .trim();
}

function formatLine(item) {
  return `${normalizeProject(item.project)} ${item.grade} ${item.ca} ${item.href}`;
}

async function fetchSignals() {
  await ensureChrome();

  await ensureChrome();
  const target = await getDebotTarget();
  const client = new CdpClient(target.webSocketDebuggerUrl);
  await client.connect();
  try {
    await client.call("Runtime.enable");
    await client.call("Page.bringToFront").catch(() => {});
    await client.call("Runtime.evaluate", {
      expression: `location.href.includes('debot.ai') || (location.href = ${JSON.stringify(CONFIG.url)})`,
    });
    await sleep(4000);
    const result = await client.call("Runtime.evaluate", {
      expression: extractionScript,
      returnByValue: true,
      awaitPromise: true,
    });
    return (result.result.value || []).map((item) => ({
      ...item,
      grade: classifySignal(item),
      seenAt: Date.now(),
    }));
  } finally {
    client.close();
  }
}

async function sendTelegram(text) {
  if (CONFIG.dryRun || !CONFIG.telegramToken || !CONFIG.telegramChatId) {
    console.log(text);
    return;
  }
  const url = `https://api.telegram.org/bot${CONFIG.telegramToken}/sendMessage`;
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      chat_id: CONFIG.telegramChatId,
      text,
      disable_web_page_preview: true,
    }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Telegram send failed: ${response.status} ${await response.text()}`);
  }
}

function pickNewSignals(signals, state) {
  return signals.filter((item) => !state.seen[item.ca]);
}

async function sendNewSignals(signals, state) {
  const fresh = pickNewSignals(signals, state);
  if (fresh.length === 0) return false;
  const text = fresh.map(formatLine).join("\n");
  await sendTelegram(text);
  for (const item of fresh) {
    state.seen[item.ca] = {
      project: item.project,
      grade: item.grade,
      href: item.href,
      firstSeenAt: Date.now(),
    };
  }
  return true;
}

async function sendSummaryIfDue(signals, state) {
  const now = Date.now();
  if (now - Number(state.lastSummaryAt || 0) < CONFIG.summaryMs) return;
  state.lastSummaryAt = now;
  const top = signals.slice(0, 20);
  const text =
    top.length > 0
      ? top.map(formatLine).join("\n")
      : "暂无新的金狗/银狗/铜狗/普通信号。";
  await sendTelegram(text);
}

async function tick(state) {
  const signals = await fetchSignals();
  const sentFresh = await sendNewSignals(signals, state);
  if (sentFresh && !state.lastSummaryAt) {
    state.lastSummaryAt = Date.now();
  }
  await sendSummaryIfDue(signals, state);
  await saveState(state);
  console.log(`[${new Date().toISOString()}] scanned=${signals.length}`);
}

async function main() {
  const state = await loadState();
  console.log(`DeBot monitor started. poll=${CONFIG.pollMs}ms summary=${CONFIG.summaryMs}ms`);
  while (true) {
    try {
      await tick(state);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`[${new Date().toISOString()}] ${error.stack || error.message}`);
    }
    await sleep(CONFIG.pollMs);
  }
}

main();

#!/usr/bin/env node

import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const CONFIG = {
  url: process.env.DEBOT_URL || "https://debot.ai/?chain=solana",
  chromePort: Number(process.env.CHROME_DEBUG_PORT || 9222),
  chromeProfileDir:
    process.env.CHROME_DEBUG_PROFILE ||
    path.join(process.cwd(), ".debot-chrome-profile"),
  pollMs: Number(process.env.DEBOT_POLL_MS || 20_000),
  summaryMs: Number(process.env.DEBOT_SUMMARY_MS || 10 * 60_000),
  statePath:
    process.env.DEBOT_STATE_PATH ||
    path.join(process.cwd(), ".debot-monitor-state.json"),
  telegramToken: process.env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN || "",
  telegramChatId: process.env.TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID || "",
  dryRun: process.env.DEBOT_DRY_RUN === "1",
};

const DOG_GRADES = [
  ["金狗", /gold|金狗|黄金|金/i],
  ["银狗", /silver|银狗|白银|银/i],
  ["铜狗", /bronze|铜狗|青铜|铜/i],
];

const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));

async function requestJson(url, options = {}) {
  const response = await fetch(url, options);
  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`${response.status} ${response.statusText}: ${await response.text()}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}

async function loadState() {
  if (!existsSync(CONFIG.statePath)) {
    return { seen: {}, lastSummaryAt: Date.now() };
  }
  try {
    return JSON.parse(await readFile(CONFIG.statePath, "utf8"));
  } catch {
    return { seen: {}, lastSummaryAt: 0 };
  }
}

async function saveState(state) {
  await mkdir(path.dirname(CONFIG.statePath), { recursive: true });
  await writeFile(CONFIG.statePath, JSON.stringify(state, null, 2));
}

function chromeExecutable() {
  const candidates = [
    "/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome",

    const match = href.match(tokenRe);
    if (!match) continue;
    const ca = match[1];

    const imgAlts = [...(card || document).querySelectorAll('img')]

`;

function classifySignal(item) {
  const haystack = `${item.text} ${item.imgAlts}`;
  for (const [grade, re] of DOG_GRADES) {
    if (re.test(haystack)) return grade;
  }
  return "普通信号";
}

function normalizeProject(project) {
  return project
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .replace(/\s+[+-]\d[\d.,]*%.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/\s+MC\s+\$.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/\s+TXs\s+.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/[：:]+$/, "")
    .trim();
}

function formatLine(item) {
  return `${normalizeProject(item.project)} ${item.grade} ${item.ca} ${item.href}`;
}

async function sendTelegram(text) {
  if (CONFIG.dryRun || !CONFIG.telegramToken || !CONFIG.telegramChatId) {
    console.log(text);
    return;
  }
  const url = `https://api.telegram.org/bot${CONFIG.telegramToken}/sendMessage`;
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      chat_id: CONFIG.telegramChatId,
      text,
      disable_web_page_preview: true,
    }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Telegram send failed: ${response.status} ${await response.text()}`);
  }
}

function pickNewSignals(signals, state) {
  return signals.filter((item) => !state.seen[item.ca]);

  if (fresh.length === 0) return false;
  const text = fresh.map(formatLine).join("\n");
  await sendTelegram(text);
  for (const item of fresh) {
    state.seen[item.ca] = {
      project: item.project,
      grade: item.grade,
      href: item.href,
      firstSeenAt: Date.now(),
    };
  }
  return true;
}

async function sendSummaryIfDue(signals, state) {
  const now = Date.now();
  if (now - Number(state.lastSummaryAt || 0) < CONFIG.summaryMs) return;
  state.lastSummaryAt = now;
  const top = signals.slice(0, 20);
  const text =
    top.length > 0
      ? top.map(formatLine).join("\n")
      : "暂无新的金狗/银狗/铜狗/普通信号。";
  await sendTelegram(text);
}

async function tick(state) {
  const signals = await fetchSignals();
  const sentFresh = await sendNewSignals(signals, state);
  if (sentFresh && !state.lastSummaryAt) {
    state.lastSummaryAt = Date.now();
  }
  await sendSummaryIfDue(signals, state);
  await saveState(state);
  console.log(`[${new Date().toISOString()}] scanned=${signals.length}`);
}

async function main() {
  const state = await loadState();
  console.log(`DeBot monitor started. poll=${CONFIG.pollMs}ms summary=${CONFIG.summaryMs}ms`);
  while (true) {
    try {
      await tick(state);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`[${new Date().toISOString()}] ${error.stack || error.message}`);
    }
    await sleep(CONFIG.pollMs);
  }
}

main();

  <instructions>
Every 10 minutes, monitor https://debot.ai/?chain=solana and analyze DeBot Solana AI signal entries. Prioritize entries that show dog-grade badges/icons: 金狗, 银狗, or 铜狗, but also include newly appearing ordinary AI signals. Detect newly appearing entries compared with the previous run when possible, and notify immediately in Chinese. Output only a copy-friendly list with project name, signal grade, CA, and DeBot token page link. Signal grade must be one of: 金狗, 银狗, 铜狗, 普通信号. Format each line exactly as: PROJECT SIGNAL_GRADE CA LINK. Use DeBot token page links when available, otherwise Pump.fun/Dex link. Do not include project introduction, risk notes, long analysis, source links section, or extra commentary. If no new entries are found, reply briefly: 暂无新的金狗/银狗/铜狗/普通信号。
  </instructions>
</heartbeat>
